- Fixed: Nested DB transaction race condition (OperationalError: cannot start a transaction within a transaction)
  - Root cause: During PHASE-1 parallel clan fetches, concurrent coroutines shared one aiosqlite connection.
    _ensure_clan_exists → _save_clan_unlocked issued an implicit auto-BEGIN (via conn.execute INSERT) and
    yielded at an await point. A second concurrent coroutine then issued an explicit execute("BEGIN"),
    which SQLite rejected because the implicit transaction was still open.
  - Occurred once in prod (2026-02-20 13:36:11) for clan #2R0GYVLJJ, causing 1-clan skip in one cycle.
  - Fix 1: Added asyncio.Lock (self._write_lock) to WarHistoryDB.__init__; all write methods now
    acquire it with acquire()/try-finally-release(), serialising every write through the event loop.
  - Fix 2: Extracted _save_clan_unlocked() (lock-free internal variant) so _ensure_clan_exists can call
    it from within an already-locked context without deadlocking.
  - Fix 3 (Pattern B correctness): save_subscriptions_for_channel and save_all_subscriptions were calling
    _ensure_clan_exists INSIDE an open BEGIN block. _save_clan_unlocked's own COMMIT would have
    prematurely committed the outer transaction if a clan was missing. Both methods now collect and
    ensure clan existence BEFORE acquiring the lock / executing BEGIN.
  - Files: qapbot/db_manager.py
  - Tests: tests/integration/test_db_manager.py — added TestConcurrentWrites with 2 concurrency tests
    (pre-existing and new-clan paths); all 203 tests pass.

- Fixed: /status CoC API success rate below 100% despite 0 errors
  - Root cause: coc.PrivateWarLog exceptions in coc_retry() incremented total_calls but neither
    successful_calls nor api_errors, so each private-warlog fetch silently reduced success rate
  - With 39 private-war-log clans queried each cycle this caused ~1-2% gap (e.g. 98.9% success)
  - Fix: count PrivateWarLog as successful_calls — the API responded correctly with a definitive
    403, this is not an API failure
  - File: qapbot/coc_health.py (coc_retry exception handler for coc.PrivateWarLog)

- Added: "Save your Buddy" feature for personal war DM notifications
  - Users can now watch CoC player tags of friends/allies via the war notification settings UI
  - New "Link Buddy" button (row 2) opens a modal to enter any CoC player tag; validates via CoC API, falls back to tag-as-name if not found

  - Buddy list displayed in notification settings message with 👥 emoji under a "Save your Buddy" headline

- Fixed: "Save your Buddy" — three bugs found during live testing
  - Bug 1 (not-found adds to DB): When CoC API cannot find the player, the buddy was silently added with the tag as name. Fixed: if API lookup fails and no local match found, reject with error message and do NOT add to DB.
  - Bug 2 (tag-only modal): Modal now accepts player name / name substring as well as a player tag (mirrors the "Link accounts" flow). New logic: (a) search all guild clan members by name substring or tag; (b) if 1 match → add directly; (c) if 2-25 matches → `BuddyPlayerSelectView` dropdown; (d) if >25 or 0 → fall back to direct CoC API tag lookup; (e) if API returns not-found → reject.
  - Bug 3 (DeprecationWarning on TextInput.label): Removed runtime `self.search_input.label = t(...)` assignment; label is now a class-level English constant ("Player Name or Tag") matching discord.py Modal lifecycle requirements. Only `placeholder` is translated at runtime.
  - New class added: `BuddyPlayerSelectView` (Select dropdown for multiple-match selections; validates and persists buddy on selection)
  - New i18n keys: `buddy_select_prompt`, `buddy_too_many_matches` (en.json + de.json)
  - Updated i18n keys: `buddy_modal_label`, `buddy_modal_placeholder`, `buddy_not_found` (all languages)
  - Files changed: `ui_notifications.py`, `translations/en.json`, `translations/de.json`

  - Root cause: migrator parsed nested format (`notified_players`/`channel_notifications`) as flat player map
  - Resulted in rows with empty `discord_id`, violating FK `notification_state.discord_id -> users.discord_id`
  - Updated `migrate_notification_state()` to parse `notified_players` correctly, skip invalid/missing-user rows,
    and replace table contents on each run (`DELETE FROM notification_state`) for true idempotent re-imports

- Fixed: subscriptions migration was not idempotent for "current year" rows
  - Root cause: SQLite UNIQUE allows multiple NULL values, so `(guild_id, channel_id, clan_tag, subscription_type, year=NULL)` duplicated on reruns
  - Updated `migrate_subscriptions()` to replace table contents (`DELETE FROM subscriptions`) before import,
    ensuring stable row counts across repeated migration runs

- Decision: Cache Write-Through Architecture Refactoring
  - Root cause: File-based bulk save pattern inherited from pre-database architecture
  - Current problem: Bot writes ALL cached data on EVERY shutdown (245 writes, ~625ms)
  - Impact: Unnecessary database load, slow shutdown, no crash protection
  - Solution: Implement write-through caching pattern
  
  **New Architecture:**
  - Write-through: Data persisted to database immediately when modified
  - No bulk saves: Shutdown requires 0ms (instant)
  - Crash resistant: Data never lost (always in sync with DB)
  - 95%+ reduction in database writes
  
  **Implementation Plan:** (qapbot/docs/CACHE_WRITETHROUGH_REFACTOR_PLAN.md)
  Phase 1: Leaderboard Messages (LOW risk, 11 modification points)
  Phase 2: Subscriptions (MEDIUM risk, 5 modification points)
  Phase 3: User Accounts (HIGH risk, 13 modification points)
  Phase 4: Clan Families (MEDIUM risk, 6 modification points)
  Phase 5: Server Config (MEDIUM risk, 20+ modification points)
  Phase 6: Notification State (LOW risk, 10 modification points)
  Phase 7: Cleanup - Remove legacy save_all() infrastructure
  
  **Estimated Timeline:** 20-25 hours total (3 weeks)
  **Status:** PLANNING phase, plan documented

  - Audit: Created PLAYER_ID_AUDIT.md with 107 occurrences across 10 files

     - create_player_notification_options()
  
  6. import_clashperk_userlist.py (3 locations)
     - find_player_in_imported_data(), import_accounts()
  
  **Fix Pattern Applied:**
  ```python
  # Fallback pattern for lookups
  current_tag = player.get("player_tag") or player.get("player_id", "")
  if current_tag == target_tag:
      # Found player
  
  # New player creation (forward-compatible)
  new_player = {
      "player_tag": tag,  # Database field
      "player_name": name
  }
  ```
  
  **Validation:**
  - Type checking: 0 errors across all 6 files ✅
  - Backwards compatible: Supports both database (player_tag) and legacy JSON (player_id)
  - Forward compatible: New entries use player_tag field

  - **Scope**: Migrate all maindata JSON configuration files to SQLite database
    * clan_name_cache.json → clans table
    * clan_families.json → clan_families + clan_family_members tables
    * user_accounts.json → users + user_players tables
    * discord_server_config.json → guild_config + guild_member_families + guild_member_clans tables

    * Added _create_maindata_schema() method with 10 tables (7 primary + 3 junction)
    * clans table: Clan name cache with subscription status, war update tracking
    * clan_families/clan_family_members: Family definitions with member relationships
    * users/user_players: User accounts with notification settings + linked player accounts
    * guild_config/guild_member_families/guild_member_clans: Discord server configuration
    * subscriptions: Channel subscriptions with clan/type/year filters
    * notification_state: War notification tracking (sent/pending notifications)
    * leaderboard_messages: Leaderboard message tracking with content hashing
    * Foreign keys: Referential integrity between users→players, guilds→families, etc.
    * Indexes: Fast lookups on discord_id, player_tag, clan_tag, guild_id

    * Subscriptions: get_subscriptions_by_channel(), save_subscriptions_for_channel(), get_all_subscriptions_dict()
    * Full CRUD operations with proper async/await patterns
    * Junction table management (user_players, guild_member_families, guild_member_clans)

    * Updated load_clan_name_cache(): Routes to DB if USE_DB_FOR_CLANS=true

    * Updated save_user_accounts(): Routes to DB if USE_DB_FOR_USERS=true

    * All methods include fallback to JSON on DB errors

    * Added 8 individual flags: USE_DB_FOR_CLANS, USE_DB_FOR_USERS, USE_DB_FOR_SUBSCRIPTIONS, 
      USE_DB_FOR_FAMILIES, USE_DB_FOR_GUILD_CONFIG, USE_DB_FOR_NOTIFICATIONS, USE_DB_FOR_LEADERBOARDS
    * Added master flag: USE_DB_FOR_MAINDATA (enables all Phase 3 tables at once)
    * Environment variables: READ from .env (default: false for all)
    * Master flag logic: If USE_DB_FOR_MAINDATA=true, enables all individual flags
  
  - **Migration Script** (qapbot/scripts/migrate_maindata_to_db.py - 649 lines):
    * Idempotent operations: INSERT OR REPLACE/INSERT OR IGNORE (safe to re-run)
    * Handles both old and new JSON formats (clan names as strings vs objects)
    * Respects foreign key dependencies: Migrates clans/families before users/subscriptions
    * Batch processing: Commits after each table migration
    * Comprehensive error handling: Logs warnings for invalid data, continues migration
    * Progress reporting: Step-by-step logging with record counts
    * Migration summary: Final statistics for all tables
  
  - **Validation Script** (qapbot/scripts/validate_maindata_migration.py - 462 lines):
    * Compares JSON files against database contents
    * Validates record counts: Ensures all JSON entries migrated to database
    * Checks data integrity: Foreign key relationships, orphaned records
    * Database integrity check: PRAGMA integrity_check for corruption detection
    * Sample validation: Tests random sample of records for correctness
    * Exit codes: 0 for success, 1 for validation failures
  
  - **Benefits**:
    * Atomic updates: No partial JSON writes during crashes
    * Referential integrity: Foreign keys prevent orphaned records
    * Faster queries: Indexed lookups vs JSON parsing (~10-100x faster)
    * Transactional consistency: Multiple related tables updated atomically
    * Concurrent reads: Multiple bot instances can read simultaneously (with WAL mode)
    * Graceful fallback: Feature flags enable instant JSON fallback on DB errors

2026-02-11 (Date)

2026-02-10 (Date)
- Fixed: Duplicate war detection incorrectly flagging all wars as duplicates
  - Root Cause 1: Each War ID appears 5 times in CSV (once per player), function counted all rows as separate wars
  - Root Cause 2: Function flagged ANY wars with same opponent as duplicates, even months apart
  - Solution 1: Changed to deduplicate War IDs before checking proximity (dict instead of list)
  - Solution 2: Only flag wars as duplicates if same opponent AND timestamps within 7 days
  - Rationale: Wars >7 days apart are legitimate different matches (CWL rematches, regular wars over time)
  - API timestamp shifts (hours/days) are true duplicates, months apart are different wars
  - Files: qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper_admin_command.py, qapbot/scripts/check_history_data_consistency.py
  - Result: /admin Check Data now correctly identifies 0 duplicates instead of falsely flagging 2563+
- Fixed: /admin Check Data output exceeds Discord 2000 character limit
  - Symptom: "400 Bad Request: Must be 2000 or fewer in length"
  - Root Cause: Truncation check happened too early, before adding current wars and field discrepancies sections
  - Solution: Moved length check to end of function with binary search to find max fitting lines
  - Added clear truncation message directing users to run check_history_data_consistency.py script
  - Files: qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper_admin_command.py (format_data_check_results function)
  - Result: Output properly truncates and stays under Discord limit
- Fixed: Bot startup hanging at various points
  - Issue 1: Lambda functions containing await expressions (SyntaxError in Python)
  - Issue 2: hasattr() checks only verified attribute existence, not truthiness (flags stayed stale)
  - Issue 3: Conditional checks for Steps 4-5 prevented execution with stale initialization flags
  - Solution: Wrapped async calls in proper async functions, fixed boolean checks, removed conditionals
  - Files: QapBot.py (on_ready, _setup_hook), qapbot/cache_manager.py (get_clan, get_player)
  - Result: Bot starts reliably and enters periodic loop without hanging
- Fixed: All Pylance type checking errors resolved

- Fixed: avgstarsbyth leaderboard showing outdated TH levels after player upgrades (BUG-FIX - PROD)
  - Symptom: Players who upgraded TH (e.g., TH17→TH18 in Nov/Dec 2025) still showed old TH17 in year 2025 leaderboards
  - Expected: Leaderboards should show the LATEST TH level within the time period
  - Root Cause 1: _merge_entries() only updated TH level when processing rows with dates STRICTLY GREATER than previous
    * Issue: Multiple wars on the same day or unsorted CSV data caused TH level updates to be skipped
    * Solution: Enhanced TH level tracking to use maximum TH on same-date wars (lines 189-207, 227-242)
  - Root Cause 2: Multi-month aggregation never updated TH_lvl for subsequent months (THE ACTUAL BUG)
    * Issue: When aggregating Jan-Dec, TH_lvl from January was kept for entire year
    * Example: Player at TH17 in Jan-Nov, upgrades to TH18 in Dec
    * Problem: First month (Jan) sets TH_lvl=17 via `.copy()`, subsequent months add stats but don't update TH
    * Solution: Track latest month in aggregation, update TH_lvl from each processed month (lines 703-717)
  - Technical Details:
    * Single-month fix: If row_date == cur["_last_date"] → use max(current_TH, new_TH)
    * Multi-month fix: If m >= aggregated[pid]["_last_month"] → update TH_lvl from month m stats
    * Added _last_month temporary tracking field, cleaned up after aggregation (line 721)
  - Files Modified:
    * QBhelperfunctions.py (lines 189-207, 227-242): Enhanced single-month TH level tracking
    * QBhelperfunctions.py (lines 703-721): Fixed multi-month aggregation TH level updates
  - Impact: All time-period leaderboards (month, year, range) now correctly show latest TH levels
  - Result: avgstarsbyth and other leaderboards accurately reflect player TH levels after upgrades
  - Testing: Year 2025 leaderboard should now show the reference player as TH18 (upgraded December)

- Fixed: Leaderboards showing only current war data after CWL war finalization (BUG-FIX - PROD)
  - Symptom: After CWL war finalized, leaderboards showed only current war instead of monthly cumulative data
  - Trigger: Only during CWL (not regular wars), specifically after war finalization routine
  - Recovery: Bot restart fixed it (reloaded history from CSV files)
  - Root Cause Analysis:
    * Feb 5 fix cleared both `clan_history` and `history_cache` after war finalization ✓
    * **ACTUAL BUG**: `get_clan_history()` only returned from in-memory cache, never reloaded from file
    * After cache invalidation → `get_clan_history()` returned `[]` (empty list)
    * Empty history → leaderboards showed only current war data
    * Bot restart worked because `load_all_clan_history()` repopulated cache at startup
  - Flow of the bug:
    1. CWL war ends and finalized to CSV ✓
    2. Cache cleared: `del CACHE.clan_history[clan_tag]` ✓
    3. Next leaderboard request → calls `_load_history_filtered()` ✓
    4. Calls `CACHE.get_clan_history(clan_tag)` ✓
    5. **BUG**: Returns `[]` because cache empty and NO FILE RELOAD ✗
    6. Filtered history is `[]`, cached as `history_cache[key] = []` ✗
    7. All subsequent leaderboards show empty history until bot restart ✗
  - Solution: Implemented lazy-loading in `get_clan_history()`
    * Now automatically loads from CSV file if cache entry missing
    * Cache invalidation no longer causes data loss
    * History immediately available after war finalization
  - Files Modified:
    * qapbot/cache_manager.py (lines 1697-1709): Added lazy-loading to `get_clan_history()`
  - Logging: Added `[CACHE-RELOAD]` debug log when lazy-loading occurs
  - Result: Leaderboards now work correctly throughout entire CWL without requiring bot restart

- Enhanced: Added defensive lazy-loading to clan_name_cache (DEFENSIVE CODING)
  - Rationale: Preventive measure in case cache invalidation added to clan_name_cache in future
  - Implementation: `get_clan_name()` reloads from JSON if cache completely empty
  - Why Only clan_name_cache:
    * Read-mostly cache (rarely modified, low risk)
    * Other JSON caches are READ-WRITE (modified during operation, saved back to files)
    * Lazy-loading READ-WRITE caches is dangerous:
      - Empty cache due to bug → lazy-load old file → overwrites unsaved in-memory changes → DATA LOSS
      - Design decision: READ-WRITE caches should FAIL LOUDLY if empty (prevents silent corruption)
  - Documentation: Added comprehensive lazy-loading strategy to CacheManager class docstring
  - Caches WITHOUT lazy-loading (by design):
    * subscriptions, user_accounts, notification_state, server_config, clan_families, leaderboard_messages
    * Empty = SystemExit(1) to prevent data corruption
  - Location: qapbot/cache_manager.py lines 832-852 (get_clan_name), 376-389 (class docstring)

2026-02-05 (Date)
- Fixed: Leaderboards freezing during CWL month transitions (Jan→Feb) (BUG-FIX - PROD)
  - Symptom: avgstars_cwl and other monthly leaderboards stopped updating mid-war, showing frozen data
  - Behavior: Data recorded correctly in JSON and history, but Discord leaderboards showed stale data
  - Recovery: Bot restart immediately fixed the issue by clearing in-memory caches
  - Root Cause: History cache invalidation was incomplete when wars finalized
  - Technical Details:
    * `_load_history_filtered()` caches filtered history by (clan_tag, month, year) tuple
    * When wars finalized to history CSV, code only cleared `CACHE.clan_history` (raw data)
    * **BUG**: `CACHE.history_cache` (filtered cache) was NOT cleared
    * Subsequent leaderboard generations used stale cached data
    * Same content → same hash → Discord messages not updated
  - Timeline observed (Feb 1-2):
    1. First Feb CWL war starts → leaderboard shows initial data ✓
    2. Attacks happen, war progresses → data written to JSON ✓
    3. War finalized to history → raw cache cleared, filtered cache NOT cleared ✗
    4. Second war starts → leaderboard regenerated with stale cached history ✗
    5. Content matches previous → hash identical → no Discord update ✗
  - Solution: Clear all filtered cache entries for clan when history is updated
  - **NOTE**: This fix was incomplete - see 2026-02-07 for full solution (lazy-loading in get_clan_history)
  - Files Modified: 
    * QBhelperfunctions.py (lines 1168-1173, 1191-1197): Added filtered cache invalidation
    * QapBot.py (lines 437-449): Added defensive temp_war_stats clearing when no war active
  - Prevention: Added logging for filtered cache invalidation to diagnose future cache issues

  - Issue: When many errors exist (e.g., 116 from API outage), users never saw recent issues

2026-01-12 (Date)
- Fixed: CRITICAL BUG - Regular wars finalized prematurely while still in preparation/in_war state
  - Problem: Wars with state='preparation' or state='in_war' being appended to history with 0 attacks/stars
  - Root cause: Faulty conditional logic in manage_war_files() line 1290: `if state == 'war_ended' or not is_cwl:`
  - Bug introduced: 2026-01-09 when fixing orphaned CWL war handling
  - Impact: ALL regular (non-CWL) wars finalized immediately regardless of state, corrupting history data
  - Examples:
    * Clan #2PR8QQ88Q: War 82QQLY2V_202601101149 saved with state='in_war', finalized with 0 missed attacks
    * Clan #L2J0C0PY: War PRVVUR2V_202601121841 saved with state='preparation', finalized with 0 attacks
  - Solution: Rewrote finalization logic with proper edge case handling
  - New logic:
    * CWL wars: Skip if state != 'war_ended' (let process_orphaned_cwl_wars() fetch final data)
    * Regular wars - state='war_ended': Finalize normally
    * Regular wars - Orphaned + state='in_war': Finalize (edge case: war ended, new war started within 300s)
    * All other cases: Skip (preparation state, or non-orphaned active wars)
  - Files: QBhelperfunctions.py (manage_war_files function, lines ~1270-1320)
  - Result: Wars finalized correctly based on state and orphan status
  - Edge case handled: Orphaned regular wars with state='in_war' finalized because no API refetch possible
  - Data cleanup needed: Remove prematurely finalized wars from history

- Fixed: Race condition causing duplicate welcome messages on bot startup
  - Root Cause: periodic_main() started as background task before on_ready() completed
    - periodic_main() immediately called main() → repost_playerregistration_messages()
    - on_ready() also called repost_playerregistration_messages() after starting periodic_main()
    - Both executions ran nearly simultaneously (within 0.23 seconds)
    - Result: Two welcome messages posted to same channel with different message IDs
  - Solution: Added initialization gate in periodic_main()
    - Wait for bot._fully_initialized flag before starting update loop
    - Ensures on_ready() completes all startup tasks before first periodic cycle
    - Prevents concurrent execution of repost_playerregistration_messages()
  - Evidence from logs:
    ```
    16:49:26,374 [INFO] Reposted playerregistration welcome message (ID: <MESSAGE_ID_1>)
    16:49:26,380 [INFO] Successfully reposted 1 playerregistration welcome messages
    16:49:26,381 [INFO] Sleeping for 300 seconds  <-- periodic_main() first cycle complete
    16:49:26,604 [INFO] Reposted playerregistration welcome message (ID: <MESSAGE_ID_2>)
    16:49:26,606 [INFO] Successfully reposted 1 playerregistration welcome messages  <-- on_ready() call
    ```
  - Impact: Eliminates duplicate welcome messages, cleaner channel presentation

- Fixed: /admin Check Logs missing "Wars Archived", "Late Attack Checks", and "Duplicate Wars Skipped" metrics
  - Root Cause: Log pattern matching used elif chain that required [INFO] level check first
    - When [INFO] was checked, subsequent [ARCHIVE], [LATE-CHECK], [DUPLICATE-SKIP] patterns were skipped
    - [LATE-CHECK] entries at DEBUG level were not being captured
  - Solution: Separated log level categorization (ERROR/WARNING) from event pattern matching
    - Log level check is now independent (if/elif for error categorization)
    - Event patterns are checked independently with if/elif chains
    - [LATE-CHECK] now counted regardless of log level
    - [ARCHIVE] "Moved" operations now properly counted
    - [DUPLICATE-SKIP] now properly counted
  - Result: All war file management metrics now show up correctly in /admin Check Logs
  - Location: qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper_admin_command.py lines 144-191

  - Impact: Ended CWL wars stayed orphaned, never finalized or moved to history

  - Location: QapBot.py Phase 2, process_orphaned_cwl_wars() parameter

  - Result: Orphaned wars finalized and moved to history in same cycle as update

- Optimized: Removed unnecessary retries for CoC API 404 errors
  - Root Cause: HTTP 404 errors were being retried 3 times even though they never resolve
  - Impact: Wasted 7+ seconds per 404 error (1s + 2s + 4s backoff delays)
  - Solution: Detect HTTP 404 status and immediately raise NotFound without retry
  - Location: coc_health.py coc_retry() line ~190
  - Result: 404 errors fail fast, reducing cycle time for inactive clans

- Refactored: War update system into true 3-phase architecture for optimal API parallelization

  - Root cause: update_clan_war_info_and_stats() contained sequential file operations (manage_war_files, temp stats calculation)
  - Solution: Split into fetch (async API) → process (sync files) → orphan fetch (async API) phases

    * Phase 1: fetch_clan_war_data() - Parallel API calls + lightweight JSON save (per clan)

    * fetch_clan_war_data(clan_tag) → Dict[str, Any] - Returns war data dict for Phase 2
    * process_clan_war_data(clan_tag, war_data) → bool - Processes fetched data sequentially
    * update_clan_war_info_and_stats() - Wrapper for backward compatibility (calls both phases)
  - Benefits: True API parallelization, cleaner separation of concerns, easier testing
  - Files: QBhelperfunctions.py (lines ~1508-1850)

  - Analysis: Complete audit of API calls per clan_tag execution
  - Result: 2-3 CoC API calls per clan_tag (typically 2, max 3 for orphaned CWL wars)
  - Always executed:
    * get_clan() via CACHE.coc_clan_cache.get_clan() - clan info with caching
    * get_current_war() - current war state (no cache, always fresh)
  - Conditionally executed:
    * get_league_war(war_tag) - ONLY for orphaned CWL wars with state != 'war_ended'
    * Scenario: Bot offline during war end, new war started, old CWL needs final data
    * Frequency: Rare edge case (~1% of cycles)
  - Performance: Efficient API usage with minimal calls (2-3 per clan vs previous 4-5)
  - Cache optimization: Clan info cached (30min TTL), war data always fresh
  - Documentation updated: [qapbot/docs/CODE_STRUCTURE.md](qapbot/docs/CODE_STRUCTURE.md) with complete API call breakdown

- Fixed: Unified war file management to prevent race condition data loss
  - Problem: War #2JY2RQPLC archived without history append (discovered in production logs)
  - Root cause: Race condition between two separate processes:
    * cache_manager.py cleanup logic moved old files by modification time
    * QBhelperfunctions.py finalization logic appended to history
    * If cleanup ran before finalization → files archived WITHOUT history append → data loss
  - Analysis: Architecture had split responsibilities creating timing vulnerability
    * Cleanup in cache_manager.py (lines 1995-2024)
    * Finalization in QBhelperfunctions.py (STEP 2, lines 1532-1648)
    * No coordination between processes → race condition possible
  - Solution: Consolidated ALL war lifecycle logic into single atomic function
    * Created: manage_war_files() in QBhelperfunctions.py (nested function)
    * Decision tree for each war file:
      1. Current war (newest + same opponent)? → SKIP
      2. Already in history? → Check late attacks → ARCHIVE
      3. Valid JSON with opponent? → FINALIZE → APPEND TO HISTORY → ARCHIVE
      4. Corrupted/incomplete? → LOG ERROR → ARCHIVE (prevent retry loops)
    * Helper: _move_to_archive() centralizes archive file operations
  - Files Modified:
    * QBhelperfunctions.py: Added manage_war_files() and _move_to_archive() (~210 lines)
    * QBhelperfunctions.py: Simplified STEP 2 from 120 lines to 5 lines
    * cache_manager.py: Removed cleanup logic (~30 lines), added redirect comment
  - Edge Cases Now Covered:
    * Current war protection (never touches active war)
    * Orphaned wars (different opponent → always finalized)
    * History duplicates (checks before appending)
    * Corrupted JSON (gracefully archived, not retried)
    * Missing opponent data (incomplete wars archived)
    * CWL wars (still fetches final data via war_tag)
    * Regular wars (finalizes with existing data)
    * Wars already in history (late attack check only)
    * Multiple rapid wars (CWL scenario - sequential processing)
  - Benefits:
    * Atomic processing - no race conditions possible
    * Single source of truth - all logic in one place
    * Idempotent - safe to run multiple times
    * Complete - handles all edge cases
    * Defensive - graceful error handling
    * Testable - clear inputs/outputs
    * Maintainable - one function to understand
  - Impact: Prevents data loss, ensures war history integrity, simplifies architecture
  - Testing Required: Verify with multiple rapid wars, corrupted files, edge cases
  - Documentation: Created UNIFIED_WAR_FILE_MANAGEMENT_PROPOSAL.md and _IMPLEMENTATION.md

- Enhanced: Orphaned CWL war handling to fetch final war data via war_tag
  - Root cause: CWL wars saved mid-battle (in_war state) may have incomplete data
  - Edge case: Bot offline during war end → new war starts → old war still in temp/ with incomplete data
  - Issue: Old war finalized with mid-battle data instead of final results (missing late attacks)
  - Solution: When orphaned CWL war detected, fetch final data from API using war_tag before finalization
  - Implementation: QBhelperfunctions.py update_clan_war_info_and_stats()
    * Detect orphaned war (different opponent than current)
    * Check if CWL (has war_tag and is_cwl=True)
    * Call get_league_war(war_tag) to fetch final war state
    * Compare old vs new data (state changes, star count changes)
    * Update JSON if changes detected (late attacks, final state)
    * Proceed with normal finalization (history + archive)
  - Handles API expiration: If API returns incomplete data, uses existing JSON
  - Benefits:
    * Ensures CWL wars archived with complete final data
    * Catches late attacks that happened after initial save
    * No changes to get_current_war() - keeps working as before
    * Only affects orphaned CWL wars (rare edge case)
  - Impact: Eliminates potential data loss for CWL wars interrupted by bot downtime

- Refactored: Consolidated war finalization logic into single location
  - Root cause: CWL fix (2026-01-03 earlier) created duplicate war finalization logic in two places
  - Issue: War history append logic existed in both cache_manager.py AND QBhelperfunctions.py
  - Risk: Duplicate processing, maintenance burden, potential edge cases missed
  - Analysis:
    * Location 1 (NEW): cache_manager.py lines 2015-2028 - emergency CWL fix (history append before archive)
    * Location 2 (EXISTING): QBhelperfunctions.py lines 1300-1400 - comprehensive finalization (orphaned wars, war_ended)
    * Both checked war_ended state and appended to history
  - Solution: Removed duplicate logic from cache_manager.py, kept comprehensive version in QBhelperfunctions.py
  - Implementation:
    * cache_manager.py: Simple cleanup archiving only (moves old files if they remain after finalization)
    * QBhelperfunctions.py: Single source of truth for war finalization (orphaned wars, war_ended, history append)
    * Execution flow: update_clan_war_info_and_stats() → save JSON → immediate JSON processing → _process_war_history()
  - Comments added to cache_manager.py explaining that finalization is handled elsewhere
  - Impact: Clean architecture, single code path for all war types, easier maintenance

- Fixed: CWL war data loss when new war starts before old war is finalized
  - Root cause: During CWL, 7 consecutive wars happen with only minutes between rounds
  - Issue: When new war starts, old war file archived without checking if written to history CSV
  - Edge case: Old war data could be lost if bot didn't finalize (write to history) before next war
  - Timeline observed:
    * 09:06 - War #2GCULRVPP vs #29YUQGCYV loaded (in_war state)
    * 14:02 - New war started, old file archived WITHOUT history check
    * 16:32 - New war #2GCULRVPP vs #2QUYU29QU loaded
  - Solution: Before archiving old war files, check if war state is "war_ended" and append to history
  - Implementation: cache_manager.py lines 1995-2030 - added history append before archive
  - Safety: Only appends if war state is ended, continues archiving even if history append fails
  - Impact: CWL wars now properly recorded in history even with rapid succession

- Major Refactor: Future-proof user_accounts.json load/save to preserve all fields
  - Root cause: Validation was creating new dicts with only hardcoded fields, losing everything else
  - Issue: Any new field (user_language, is_primary, future additions) would be deleted on restart
  - Old approach: Extract specific fields → create new dict with only those fields → lose everything else
  - New approach: Copy full dict → validate required fields → set defaults for optional → preserve all other fields
  - Implementation:
    * User level: Start with entry.copy(), validate display_name and players, preserve user_language and all future fields
    * Player level: Start with p.copy(), validate player_id and player_name, set defaults for verified/is_primary, preserve all future fields
    * setdefault() for optional fields ensures they exist without overwriting if already present
  - Impact: Future fields automatically preserved without code changes - no more data loss on restart
  - File changes: cache_manager.py load_user_accounts() with new validation strategy

- Fixed: is_primary attribute deleted from user_accounts.json after bot restart
  - Root cause: load_user_accounts() validation was filtering out is_primary field
  - Issue: Only preserved player_id, player_name, and verified - discarded all other fields
  - Impact: is_primary flag lost on every bot restart, breaking primary account functionality
  - Solution: Added is_primary to preserved fields in load_user_accounts() validation
  - Implementation: cache_manager.py line 771 - extract and preserve is_primary (default False)
  - File changes: cache_manager.py load_user_accounts() and updated docstrings

- Fixed: InteractionResponded error in ClanManagementLinkAccountView submit button
  - Root cause: Mismatched interaction response handling when admin_override is auto-detected vs passed as parameter
  - Issue: When admin status detected automatically, interaction was deferred, but code tried to use response.send_message()
  - Flow problem:
    * admin_override=False (default) → interaction deferred
    * Admin detected → admin_override set to True
    * Success handler checked admin_override but didn't check if interaction was deferred
    * Tried to use response.send_message() after defer → InteractionResponded error
  - Solution: Track interaction_deferred flag and use appropriate send method (followup vs response)
  - Implementation: Updated ui_components.py _on_submit() to track defer state and choose correct response method
  - Impact: Admin override now works correctly without interaction errors

- Fixed: ImportError in ClanManagementLinkAccountView - SERVER_ADMIN not found
  - Root cause: Incorrect import - tried to import SERVER_ADMIN from qapbot.config where it doesn't exist
  - Issue: Used wrong pattern - should be CONFIG.server_admin (following existing codebase pattern)
  - Solution: Changed import from `from qapbot.config import SERVER_ADMIN` to `from qapbot.config import CONFIG`
  - Implementation: Updated ui_components.py line 5905 to use CONFIG.server_admin
  - Impact: Admin override check now works correctly

  - Implementation: Modified ClanManagementLinkAccountView._on_submit() to detect admin status and set admin_override=True
  - Impact: Admins can now properly override unverified player links without needing API tokens

  Documentation:

  Benefits:

- Major Enhancement: Implemented comprehensive user language preference storage system
  - Root cause: Discord API doesn't provide user locale when fetching users by ID, only during interactions
  - Solution: Store and auto-update user language preferences captured from interaction context
  - Implementation:
    * Added "user_language" field to user_accounts.json (en/de supported)
    * Created update_user_metadata() in cache_manager.py - auto-updates display_name and user_language
    * Created ensure_user_metadata() in cache_manager.py - lazy-loads missing metadata from API
    * Modified _get_user_language() in war_notifications.py - now async, reads from stored preferences
    * Updated _send_aggregated_war_reminder_dm() - updates user metadata during notification send
    * Added update_user_metadata_from_interaction() helper in ui_components.py
    * Integrated metadata updates in all WelcomeView button handlers (Link Account, War Notifications, API Verification, My Accounts)
  - Data Flow:
    1. User interacts with bot → interaction.locale captured
    2. update_user_metadata() extracts locale, maps to language code (de_* → 'de', others → 'en')
    3. Stored in user_accounts.json alongside display_name and other user data
    4. War notifications read stored language preference
    5. Messages formatted in user's preferred language
  - Auto-Update Rules:
    * Compare cached values (display_name, user_language) with fresh API/interaction data
    * Update cache and save if any values changed
    * Triggered on: user interactions, fetch_user() calls, cache reads with missing data
  - Fallback Chain:
    1. Stored user_language in cache (primary)
    2. Fetch from Discord if missing
    3. Default to English if unavailable
  - Impact: War reminder DMs now reliably multi-lingual based on user's Discord language setting
  - Testing: German Discord users receive "KRIEGS-ERINNERUNG", English users receive "WAR REMINDER"

- Fixed: Welcome message account verification workflow now properly updates after successful API verification
  - Root cause: VerifyAccountModal.on_submit() was calling show_overview() which consumed the interaction
  - After deferring modal submission, must use parent_view.original_interaction.edit_original_response()
  - Impact: "My Accounts" button now correctly refreshes the account list after verification
  - Implementation: Removed show_overview() call, used parent_view pattern to update original message

    * Problem description with WRONG example

  - Purpose: Prevent these issues from recurring in future implementations

- Fixed: Duplicate "Link Player Account" message during admin override in clan management
  - Root cause: Admin override confirmation callback used confirm button's interaction
  - When _on_submit called with admin_override=True, it deferred the confirm button interaction
  - This created a new ephemeral message instead of editing the original link view message
  - Fix implemented:
    1. Don't defer interaction when admin_override=True (it's the confirm button)
    2. Use self.link_view_message.edit() instead of interaction.edit_original_response()
    3. Send success response to confirm button interaction
  - Result: Link view message updates in-place without duplication

- Fixed: Discord interaction response handling for modal chaining and API token ownership conflicts
  - Root cause #1: ApiTokenOwnershipModal used use_followup=False, causing webhook expiration
  - Root cause #2: PlayerSubstringModal didn't defer, causing "cannot send modal after modal" error
  - Root cause #3: complete_account_linking_flow tried to show modal when response already used
  - Root cause #4: process_player_registration error handling used response.send_message after defer
  - Fixes implemented:
    1. ApiTokenOwnershipModal.on_submit() now defers interaction immediately
    2. PlayerSubstringModal.on_submit() now defers interaction immediately
    3. All modal paths now use use_followup=True after deferring
    4. complete_account_linking_flow checks interaction.response.is_done() before showing modal
    5. If response already used, sends followup message with /myaccounts command instruction
    6. process_player_registration error handling checks is_done() and uses followup if needed
  - Result: All modal flows now work correctly without webhook/response errors
  - Files modified:
    * qapbot/ui_components.py - added defer() to modal submissions, changed all paths to use followup
    * qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py - added is_done() checks before showing modal and in error handling

- Added: CoC API Token Override for account ownership proof (Rule 4)
  - Feature: ANY user can now re-link ANY account (verified or unverified) by proving ownership with CoC API token
  - Rationale: CoC API token is cryptographic proof of rightful account ownership
  - Implementation flow:
    1. User attempts to link player already registered to another user
    2. System detects conflict via get_any_player_owner()
    3. ApiTokenOwnershipModal prompts user to enter CoC API token
    4. validate_api_token_for_player() validates token against CoC API
    5. If valid: Player removed from previous owner, linked to new user in VERIFIED state
    6. Previous owner receives DM explaining removal with ownership proof context
  - Security: API token validation overrides all other account protection rules
  - Result: Player automatically verified when proven via API token
  - New functions:
    * get_any_player_owner() - checks if player linked to ANY user (verified or unverified)
    * validate_api_token_for_player() - validates CoC API token for player ownership
  - Modified functions:
    * _link_player_to_user() - added api_token_override and api_token_user_id parameters
    * complete_account_linking_flow() - added STEP 0 for API token ownership check
    * process_player_registration() - added api_token handling for ownership proof
  - New UI component:
    * ApiTokenOwnershipModal - modal for API token entry when ownership conflict detected
  - Translation keys added:
    * api_token_required, api_token_placeholder, api_token_invalid
    * api_token_verified_success, api_token_removed_dm
  - Files modified:
    * qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py - core account protection and linking logic
    * qapbot/ui_components.py - ApiTokenOwnershipModal
    * qapbot/translations/en.json - translation keys
    * .github/copilot-instructions.md - Rule 4 marked as IMPLEMENTED

- Fixed: Account protection in clan management now properly checks if player is VERIFIED by another user
  - Root cause: Previous implementation incorrectly checked target user's verified accounts instead of player's ownership
  - Correct implementation (matching /link_account logic):
    * Uses get_verified_player_owner() to check if player is verified by DIFFERENT Discord user
    * Shows verified owner's display name (not Discord mention)
    * Warning message displays: player info + current verified owner's name
    * Clear explanation that linking will REMOVE player from verified owner's account
    * Requires bot admin + explicit confirmation via ClanManagementAdminOverrideView
  - Protection flow:
    1. Check if player is verified by another user
    2. If yes: Show "Admin Override Required" warning with Cancel (default) / Confirm Override buttons
    3. If Cancel: No changes made
    4. If Confirm: Proceeds with admin_override=True flag through complete_account_linking_flow
    5. Backend removes player from previous owner, sends DM notification, links to new user
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (ClanManagementLinkAccountView._on_submit, new ClanManagementAdminOverrideView class)
  - Pattern: Identical security model as /link_account command - only VERIFIED accounts are protected

- Changed: User input errors now logged as INFO with [USER ERROR] prefix instead of ERROR level
  - Root cause: "Failed to fetch player" errors from invalid player tags were logged as ERROR (system issue)
  - Fix: Changed logging.error to logging.info with "[USER ERROR]" prefix for user input failures
  - Rationale: User input errors are expected and shouldn't pollute ERROR logs
  - Example: `2026-01-02 14:31:52,184 [INFO] [USER ERROR] Failed to fetch player #2GQP89: notFound (status code: 404)`
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (ManualPlayerTagModal and ManualUserIDModal exception handlers)

- Fixed: /admin List All Subscriptions now excludes family member clans from "Unsubscribed Clans" list
  - Root cause: Only checked direct subscriptions, ignored clans indirectly subscribed via family
  - Fix: Added family member clan collection when family is subscribed (subscribed_clans.update(family_clans))
  - Result: Unsubscribed clans list now only shows truly unsubscribed clans
  - File modified: qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper_admin_command.py (list_all_subscriptions function)

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Duplicate API Token Modal Consolidated)
- Fixed: Two different API token entry modals caused confusion
  - Issue: User saw duplicate "Verify Account" modal in different contexts
  - **Root Cause**: Architectural mismatch in modal usage
    * VerifyAccountModal: Designed for EXISTING unverified players in account
    * ApiVerificationPromptView: Used VerifyAccountModal for NEW player registration (wrong!)
    * Result: Modal expected player to exist, but player wasn't registered yet → confusion
  - **Solution**: Created dedicated ApiTokenEntryModal for new player registration with verification
    * Purpose: Register NEW player with immediate API verification
    * Flow: Takes player_tag + player_name, gets API token, calls complete_account_linking_flow()
    * Consolidation: Now each modal has a clear, distinct purpose
  - **Modal Purposes (Clarified)**:
    * ApiTokenEntryModal: NEW player registration WITH verification (from API prompt)
    * VerifyAccountModal: Verify EXISTING unverified player (from account management)
    * PlayerSubstringModal: Player search/selection with optional token (initial registration)
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (created ApiTokenEntryModal, updated ApiVerificationPromptView)
  - Impact: Clear separation of concerns, no more modal confusion

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Missing guild_id in Verification Functions)
- Fixed: Verification error messages still showing in English despite translation keys
  - Issue: "Could not verify ownership of Killer (#G0CQ2PYG). Please check that:..." in English
  - Root cause: _verify_player_with_token() and verify_and_update_player() lacked guild_id parameter
  - Without guild_id, t() function defaulted to English instead of using server language
  - Fix: Added guild_id parameter to both functions and updated all callers
  - **Functions Updated**:
    * _verify_player_with_token(): Added guild_id parameter, passed to all t() calls
    * verify_and_update_player(): Added guild_id parameter, passed to _verify_player_with_token()
  - **Callers Updated**:
    * complete_account_linking_flow(): Passes guild_id from interaction
    * VerifyAccountModal.on_submit(): Passes guild_id from interaction

  - Impact: Verification messages now properly translated based on server language setting

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - CoC API Lookup Error Webhook Expiration)
- Fixed: "Unknown Webhook" error when entering invalid player tag in registration modal
  - Issue: Edge case where weird string interpreted as player ID caused CoC API NotFound error
  - Error sequence: 
    1. User enters invalid string in PlayerSubstringModal
    2. Interpreted as direct player tag (#PLAYERTAG format)
    3. CoC API lookup fails with coc.errors.NotFound
    4. Error handler tried to use interaction.followup.send()
    5. Webhook expired because modal interaction hadn't been responded to yet
  - Root cause: process_player_registration() incorrectly used followup instead of response for modal context
  - Fix: Always use interaction.response.send_message() for CoC API lookup failures
  - Translation: Added 'playerregistration.coc_api_lookup_failed' key (English/German)
  - Files modified: 
    * qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py (process_player_registration function)
    * qapbot/translations/en.json, de.json (new error message key)
  - Impact: Graceful error handling for invalid player tags, no more webhook crashes

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Modal Label Translation Limitation)
- Fixed: Removed TextInput label translation to avoid discord.py deprecation warnings
  - Issue: discord.py 2.6.4 deprecates modifying TextInput.label after creation
  - Solution: Keep TextInput labels in English (technical requirement)
  - Workaround: Only translate placeholders (provides sufficient context)
  - Rationale: Modal titles and field labels must remain English due to discord.py Modal lifecycle
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (PlayerSubstringModal, VerifyAccountModal)
  - Documentation: Updated copilot-instructions.md with i18n limitations

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Modal Class Definition Pattern Fix)
- Fixed: Player registration "Unknown interaction" error caused by incorrect Modal class pattern
  - Root cause: Changed Modal class from discord.py's required pattern during i18n implementation
  - OLD PATTERN (working): `class Modal(discord.ui.Modal, title="...")` with TextInput as class attributes
  - BROKEN PATTERN: `class Modal(discord.ui.Modal):` with TextInput created in __init__() via add_item()
  - Impact: Discord.py requires TextInputs as class attributes for proper Modal lifecycle initialization
  - When TextInputs are instance attributes, interaction token becomes invalid before on_submit is called
  - Error: discord.errors.NotFound: 404 Not Found (error code: 10062): Unknown interaction
  - Fix: Reverted to class-attribute pattern with hardcoded English titles at class definition
  - i18n Solution: Translate TextInput labels/placeholders in __init__() after Modal instantiation
  - Affected Classes: PlayerSubstringModal, VerifyAccountModal
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (both Modal class definitions)
  - Result: Modal interactions now work correctly with translated TextInput fields

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Reverted Incorrect Fixes)
- Reverted: Removed defer() workaround from PlayerSubstringModal.on_submit()
  - Previous assumption: Interaction timeout due to slow processing (INCORRECT)
  - Actual root cause: Modal class definition pattern broke interaction lifecycle
  - Removed: interaction.response.defer() at start of on_submit
  - Changed back: All followup.send() reverted to response.send_message()
  - Changed back: process_player_registration(..., use_followup=True) → use_followup=False
  - Impact: Cleaner code, no unnecessary defer/followup pattern
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (PlayerSubstringModal.on_submit)

2025-12-31 (Production Hotfix - Webhook Expiration Handling)
- Fixed: Player registration error when webhook expires (404 Unknown Webhook)
  - Root cause: complete_account_linking_flow() attempted to send followup message after webhook expired (>15 minutes)
  - Error occurred in line 1220 of QBdiscocmdshelper.py when showing API verification prompt
  - User saw German error message: "Etwas ist schiefgelaufen. Bitte versuche es noch einmal."
  - Fix: Added try/except block with discord.errors.NotFound handling
  - Behavior: Now logs warning and attempts to inform user via response message
  - Impact: Graceful error handling prevents silent failures, user gets clear feedback to retry
  - Files modified: qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py (complete_account_linking_flow function)

- QBdiscordcmds.py: Commands updated to pass guild_id
- QapBot.py: Welcome message function uses i18n
- .github/copilot-instructions.md: Added comprehensive i18n guidelines

i18n Guidelines Established:
- User-facing Discord messages: TRANSLATE
- Terminal output and logs: DO NOT TRANSLATE (keep English)
- Leaderboard column headers: DO NOT TRANSLATE (consistency across languages)
- Number formatting: DO NOT TRANSLATE (no locale-specific formatting)
- All translation keys use dot notation (e.g., "playerregistration.button_link_account")
- Variables use format syntax (e.g., t("welcome", server_name="My Server"))

Technical Implementation:
- Translation files: qapbot/translations/{language_code}.json
- Language preferences: CACHE.server_config[guild_id]["language"]
- Default language: English ("en")
- Guild context: Automatically passed via guild_id parameter
- DM context: Falls back to English when guild context unavailable

Next Steps for i18n Expansion:
- Add more languages (French, Spanish, etc.) by creating new JSON files
- Translate additional commands as needed
- Translate leaderboard explanatory text (keep headers as-is)
- Add language-specific help documentation

Production Deployment Notes:
- No breaking changes to existing functionality
- All existing English text maintained as default
- Users in servers without language preference see English
- German translations fully tested and ready
- Language can be changed at any time via /admin command without bot restart

  - Purpose: Hide incomplete features in production while keeping them visible in DEV mode
  - Implementation:
    * Added ENABLE_FAMILIES_MODE and ENABLE_ROLES_MODE constants in qapbot/config.py
    * Both default to False (hidden in PROD)
    * Modes are always visible when CONFIG.is_dev_mode is True
    * _add_mode_select() conditionally includes modes based on toggles
  - Usage:
    * DEV mode: All modes visible for testing
    * PROD mode: Only enabled modes visible to users
    * To enable in PROD: Change constants to True in config.py
  - Activation Path: Simply set ENABLE_FAMILIES_MODE = True to activate in production
  - Files modified: qapbot/config.py (feature toggle constants), qapbot/ui_components.py (ClanManagementView._add_mode_select)

- Changed: Average stars per attack calculation for all avgstars leaderboard modes
  - Previous behavior: Calculated as Stars / (Attacks + Missed_Attacks), treating missed attacks as 0-star attacks
  - New behavior: Calculated as Stars / Attacks, only counting attacks actually made

  - Impact: Add clan button failed to refresh view, showing error in logs
  - Flow Analysis:
    * Single match: add_clan_by_search sends ephemeral message → interaction used
    * API fetch: add_clan_by_search defers then sends followup → interaction used
    * Multiple matches: Modal shows selection → selection callback defers/deletes → interaction used
  - Fix: Manually rebuild and edit parent_view.sent_message instead of using _refresh_view()
  - Applied to: AddClanModal.on_submit() and on_clan_selected() callback
  - Rationale: _refresh_view() expects fresh interaction.response, but all paths use interaction first
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py (AddClanModal, add_clan_by_search)

- Added: ClashPerk user import script (qapbot/import_clashperk_userlist.py)
  - Purpose: Import Discord-to-CoC account mappings from ClashPerk bot's clan management embed
  - Features:
    * Dynamic message fetching with interactive prompts (Guild ID, Channel ID, Message ID)
    * Auto-detects clan tag from embed author field
    * Uses CoC API as authoritative source for player names and tags
    * Leftmost matching algorithm for parsing lines with multiple player names
    * Unicode apostrophe normalization (U+2018, U+2019 → ASCII) for accurate matching
    * **Verified status detection**: Detects :Verified: emoji (ID 803884634768408577) and imports verified=True
    * **Smart overwrite logic**: Only upgrades verified status (False → True), never downgrades
    * **Two-step confirmation**: Preview changes, then ask user to confirm before writing
    * Compares with existing user_accounts.json to skip duplicates
    * Automatic timestamped backup before modifications (user_accounts_backup_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS.json)
    * Proper async cleanup (asyncio.sleep, gc.collect) to avoid unclosed connector warnings
  - Results: Successfully parsed 49/49 linked players with 100% accuracy, 9 verified accounts detected
  - Dependencies: discord.py, coc.py, requires DEV credentials in .env
  - Files created: qapbot/scripts/import_clashperk_userlist.py

2025-12-26
- Major Enhancement: Player registration workflow redesign with improved UX and heuristic filtering
  - Root cause: Old flow forced clan selection first, even for single-clan guilds or unique player names
  - Redesigned flow: Substring modal FIRST (searches all clans) → Clan selection ONLY if >25 matches
  - Implementation:
    * PlayerSubstringModal now receives guild_id and all guild-subscribed clans at creation
    * Modal searches ALL clans simultaneously for name/tag substring matches
    * If ≤25 matches → Show player selection directly (no clan selection needed)
    * If >25 matches → Show clan selection dropdown to narrow down (new _clan_filter_callback)
    * After clan selection, if still >25 matches → Apply is_current_member heuristic filter
  - Heuristic filtering: When >25 matches after clan selection, filter to current clan members only
    * Rationale: Most users want to link current players, not historical war participants
    * Fallback: If no current members in matches, keep all matches (edge case handling)
    * Result: 31 matches → 14 current members (dramatically improved UX in testing)
  - Tag matching: Changed from substring to exact match only (substr == tag not substr in tag)
    * Prevents false positives (41 false matches → 10 correct matches in testing)
    * Name matching still uses substring for flexibility
  - Benefits:
    * Faster UX for single-clan guilds (no unnecessary clan selection step)
    * Faster UX for unique player names (direct to player selection)
    * Reduced dropdown clutter from historical players
    * Clan selection only shown when actually needed (>25 matches)
  - Files modified: qapbot/ui_components.py, qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py

  - Affected flows: Welcome message modal, /myaccounts add, /link_account admin command

    * Added target_user_id parameter (defaults to interaction.user.id for self-registration)

    * Built-in CoC API player lookup fallback for unknown clans (edge case support)

    * Eliminated ~30 lines of duplicate CoC API lookup code from LinkAccountUserSelectView
    * Removed redundant validation and error handling across three separate paths
    * Single source of truth for: tag validation → CoC API lookup → registration → verification → notifications
  - Benefits:
    * Reduced testing complexity (3 different entry points → 1 configurable entry point)
    * Fewer edge cases to handle (CoC API errors, validation failures now in one place)
    * Easier maintenance (one function to update instead of three separate code paths)
    * All flows automatically benefit from future enhancements
  - Workflow path (identical for all three):
    ```
    process_player_registration()
      ├─ Tag validation & normalization
      ├─ CoC API player lookup (if name unknown/missing)
      ├─ Error handling & message cleanup
      └─ complete_account_linking_flow(target_user_id, show_api_prompt)

            ├─ API Verification (conditional based on show_api_prompt)
            └─ Notification Check (check_and_prompt_war_notifications)
    ```
  - Result: Maximum code reuse, minimal configuration, streamlined testing

- Added: New leaderboard mode "avgstarsbyth" - Average stars per attack grouped by TH level

  - Usage examples:
    * `/leaderboard mode:avgstarsbyth` - Current month all clans

- Enhanced: Leaderboard sorting to use TH level as secondary criterion
  - Root cause: User request for more meaningful tie-breaking when players have equal primary stats
  - Implementation:
    * Updated all 12 MODE_REGISTRY sort_key functions in qapbot/formatting.py
    * Sorting hierarchy: Primary criterion → TH level (high to low) → Player name (alphabetical)
  - Sorting by Mode:
    * attack/currentwar: Stars → TH → Name
    * avgstars: Avg stars/atk → TH → Total stars → Name
    * attackdefratio: Atk/Def ratio → TH → Name
    * defense: Def stars/war → TH → Name
    * missedattacks: Missed attacks → TH → Name
    * All CWL modes follow same pattern as standard modes
  - Rationale:
    * Higher TH players achieving same stats as lower TH players deserve lower ranking (harder matchup)
    * Provides consistent, meaningful tie-breaking across all leaderboard modes
    * Eliminates arbitrary alphabetical-only sorting for equal performers
  - Example: Two players with 24 stars → TH17 ranks above TH15 → If both TH17, alphabetical
  - Result: More strategic and fair leaderboard rankings that account for townhall difficulty

    * Updated render_leaderboard() to display TH level in all row outputs
    * Column format: "TH" header with 2-char width for values (e.g., "16", "15", "17")
    * Position: Immediately after Player name, before Stars column
  - Modes Updated:
    * Standard modes: currentwar, attack, avgstars, attackdefratio, defense, missedattacks
    * CWL modes: attack_cwl, avgstars_cwl, attackdefratio_cwl, defense_cwl, missedattacks_cwl
    * Sample output: "PlayerOne      16    24       8        3.00         3"
  - Design Decision: TH level is always shown (not configurable)
    * Rationale: TH level is fundamental context for interpreting war stats
    * Simplicity: No configuration state to manage, consistent behavior
    * Alignment: No per-display toggles exist in current architecture
  - Result: All leaderboards now show TH level for strategic analysis and performance context

2025-12-24
- Fixed: Unverified accounts can no longer be registered by multiple users simultaneously
  - Issue: Unverified accounts could be "claimed" by multiple users, creating duplicate registrations
  - Root cause: Registration logic only checked for verified accounts owned by others
  - Security impact: User A with unverified account could have it "stolen" by User B registering same player
  - Fix: Added STEP 0.5 in process_player_registration() to detect and remove unverified duplicates
  - Behavior:
    * Before registering, scan all user accounts for unverified duplicates of the same player_id
    * If found, remove the unverified player from the other user's account
    * Send DM to affected user explaining the removal and how to verify accounts
    * Log security event for audit trail
  - Result: Each player can only be registered (verified or unverified) by ONE user at a time
  - DM notification includes:
    * Which account was removed
    * Why it was removed (another user registered it)
    * How to verify accounts to prevent this (/myaccounts verify command)
    * Instructions to get CoC API token
- Fixed: Link account button failing when only one clan available in guild
  - Issue: AttributeError: 'Webhook' object has no attribute 'send_modal'
  - Root cause: Attempted to use interaction.followup.send_modal() which doesn't exist in Discord API
  - Context: Single-clan optimization path tried to send message before opening modal
  - Fix: Removed intermediate message and directly call interaction.response.send_modal()
  - Discord constraint: Modals must be sent via interaction.response.send_modal() as first/only response
  - Result: Link account button works correctly for guilds with one or multiple clans

  - Root cause: Legacy temp CSV backup code referenced function removed during Phase 6 JSON-only migration
  - Impact: Wars ending between code deployment and fix were archived but not appended to history CSV files
  - Fix: Removed obsolete temp CSV backup code block (lines 250-259 in QBcsvhandling.py)
  - Verification: History CSV backup code (lines 269-276) remains functional and continues creating rolling backups
  - Result: War end handling now works correctly - wars properly appended to history and moved to archive
- Implemented: Player war notification system for attack reminders
  - Purpose: Automatically send Discord DM reminders to players who haven't completed their clan war attacks
  - Architecture:
    * New module: qapbot/war_notifications.py - Core notification logic and DM sending
    * New data file: data/notification_state.json - Tracks sent notifications to prevent duplicates
    * Integration: check_wars_for_notifications() called in periodic_main() loop after war updates
  - Configuration (qapbot/config.py):
    * NOTIFICATION_HOURS_BEFORE_END: Default 4 hours (configurable via environment variable)
    * NOTIFICATION_BATCH_DELAY: 2 seconds between DMs for rate limiting
    * NOTIFICATION_MAX_RETRIES: 1 retry on transient failures
  - User Account Schema Updates:
    * Added notification_settings to user_accounts.json structure
    * Fields: war_reminders (bool), notification_mode (str), hours_before_end (int)
    * Order: display_name -> notification_settings -> players (per user request)
  - Notification State Structure (notification_state.json):
    * war_id (clan_tag_opponent_tag) -> notified_players dict
    * Tracks: player_tag, player_name, discord_id, notification_time, attacks_remaining
    * Prevents duplicate notifications (once mode) and tracks timing (repeated mode)
  - Discord Commands:
    * /notify action:Subscribe - Enable war reminders for all registered players
    * /notify action:Configure - Interactive UI to configure notification settings (war type and mode)
    * /notify action:Settings - View current notification preferences
    * /notify action:Unsubscribe - Disable war reminders
    * /admin action:TEST_NOTIFY - Manual test for bot admin (defaults to the home clan, limited to the bot admin)
  - UI Components (qapbot/ui_components.py):
    * NotificationTypeView - Select war type filter (All Wars / CWL Only)
    * NotificationModeView - Select notification frequency (Once / Repeated)
    * NotificationConfigView - Main config menu with buttons for both settings
    * All buttons use blue background (ButtonStyle.primary) for consistency
    * Detailed explanations shown before user selection
  - Notification Features:
    * War Type Filtering:
      - all_wars (default): Notifications for all war types (regular, CWL, friendly)
      - cwl_only: Only Clan War League wars trigger notifications
    * Notification Modes:
      - repeated (default): Multiple reminders with adaptive intervals:
        • Every 2 hours (when > 2h remaining)
        • Every hour (when 0.5h - 2h remaining)
        • Every 30 minutes (when < 0.5h remaining)
      - once: Single notification when threshold reached (no repeat reminders)
  - Message Formatting:
    * Aggregated by Discord user (one DM per user with all CoC accounts)
    * Uses Discord display name in greeting
    * Tabular list of accounts with remaining attacks
    * Visual separators (━━━) for clarity between consecutive messages
    * Shows: total attacks across accounts, clan vs opponent, time remaining
  - Notification Logic (war_notifications.py):
    * _get_active_wars(): Loads war data from data/temp/*.json files
    * _get_hours_until_war_end(): Parses Timestamp strings from coc.py
    * _get_players_needing_reminders(): Filters by attacks, subscription, war type
    * _should_send_notification(): Implements once/repeated mode with timing intervals
    * _should_notify_for_war_type(): Checks war type filter (all_wars vs cwl_only)
    * _send_aggregated_war_reminder_dm(): Sends consolidated DM per Discord user
    * _record_notification(): Updates notification_state.json with timestamp
    * test_send_notification(): Admin testing function (bypasses state tracking, test mode only)
  - Admin Enhancements:
    * /admin TEST_NOTIFY: Tests notifications for clan #L2J0C0PY
      - Bypasses notification_state check for repeated testing
      - Limited to the configured bot admin (SERVER_ADMIN)
      - Removed clan_tag parameter (defaults to the bot admin's clan)
    * /admin CHECK_LOGS: Now shows "Notification Messages Sent" count
      - Scans logs for "✅ Sent war reminder" entries
      - Added to Event Summary section
  - Cache Manager Integration (qapbot/cache_manager.py):
    * Added notification_state property with load/save methods
    * Fixed load_user_accounts() to preserve notification_settings field
    * Ensures proper field ordering in user account data
  - Helper Functions (qapbot/QBdiscocmdshelper.py):
    * format_notification_settings(): Display user's current notification config with mode descriptions
    * update_notification_subscription(): Enable/disable with proper field ordering
  - Error Handling:
    * Individual DM failures don't block other notifications
    * Discord.Forbidden: Logs warning for blocked/disabled DMs
    * Discord.HTTPException: Logs HTTP errors with retry logic
    * Missing player data logged for transparency
    * War data loading errors gracefully skipped
  - Performance & Rate Limiting:
    * Batched notifications with configurable delays between DMs
    * Groups players by discord_id to minimize total messages
    * Async processing for non-blocking operation
    * State saved after each notification batch
  - Status Command Enhancement:
    * /status now shows "Notification Subscriptions: X (Y coc accounts in total)"
    * Counts users with war_reminders=True and their total player count
  - Implementation Notes:
    * Subscription system implemented from the start (not phased)
    * No hard-coded Discord IDs except for test mode restriction
    * Removed "default" notification_mode - only "once" and "repeated" supported
    * Test command bypasses state tracking for repeated testing convenience
    * notification_settings field order: display_name -> notification_settings -> players
  - Benefits:
    * Flexible notification preferences per user (war type and frequency)
    * Aggregated messages reduce Discord notification spam
    * Repeated mode helps forgetful players with timely reminders
    * CWL-only mode useful for competitive clans focusing on league wars
    * Integrates seamlessly with existing player registration system
  - Result: Complete notification system with full configurability ready for production

  - Root cause: DEBUG level enabled excessive "Total length of..." messages drowning out useful logs
  - Solution: Preserved logging code with clear comment for future debugging, but disabled by default
  - Benefit: Clean logs at DEBUG level while maintaining diagnostic capability
- Fixed: Race condition causing war_ended JSON files to remain in temp/ directory
  - Root cause: JSON save happened AFTER immediate processing check, so newly saved files weren't processed until next cycle
  - Problem: War_ended files would be saved, then next cycle would process them, then CoC API would return same war_ended data and save again
  - Solution: Reordered operations - save JSON immediately after API call, THEN do immediate processing
  - Flow: API call → Save JSON to temp/ → Immediate processing → Move to archive
  - Removed: Duplicate WAR_ENDED STATE HANDLING section that was redundant after immediate processing
  - Removed: Archive existence check before save (simplified - let file cycle through save→process→archive within 24h threshold)
  - Result: War_ended files are immediately processed and moved to archive, no longer reappear in temp/
  - Architecture: Single place for JSON save (after API), single place for war end processing (immediate after save)
  - Benefit: Cleaner code with no duplicate logic, predictable file lifecycle, correct file counts in temp/
- Improved: War finalization logging to distinguish actual history changes from archive refreshes
  - Root cause: CoC API continues returning war_ended data after war ends, causing repeated JSON processing
  - Problem: Old logging showed "[ARCHIVE] Moved..." for every processing cycle, making it appear as new war endings
  - Solution: Added distinct log markers based on whether history was actually changed:
    - [WAR-ENDED]: New war finalized and added to history
    - [WAR-UPDATED]: Existing war updated with late attacks
    - [ARCHIVE-REFRESH]: JSON refreshed in archive without history changes
  - Updated scan_qapbot_logs.py to count only [WAR-ENDED] and [WAR-UPDATED] as actual war events
  - Result: Clear distinction between meaningful war endings (1-5 per day) vs routine refreshes (hundreds per day)
  - Benefit: Accurate monitoring and troubleshooting of war finalization operations

2025-12-21
- Fixed: CRITICAL BUG - Ongoing wars incorrectly finalized when current API war is war_ended
  - Root cause: Orphaned JSON check used current API war state to decide if same-opponent JSON should be processed
  - Problem: If API returns war_ended, ALL matching JSONs processed regardless of their actual state (in_war/preparation)
  - Solution: Load each JSON file and check its internal 'state' field before deciding to process
  - New logic: Process JSON if (1) different opponent = orphaned OR (2) JSON's own state == 'war_ended'
  - Result: Only wars that are actually ended in their JSON files get finalized, ongoing wars remain untouched
  - Impact: Prevented data corruption from premature finalization of active wars
- Refactored: Consolidated all war history operations into single _process_war_history() helper
  - Root cause: Three separate code paths (orphaned JSON, war_already_in_history, _finalize_war) all did same thing
  - Solution: Single helper function handles: load JSON, check history, update/append, move to archive, invalidate cache
  - Eliminated: ~150 lines of duplicate logic across three different locations
  - Result: Single source of truth for history operations, cleaner error handling, easier maintenance
  - Benefits: One function to test/debug instead of three, consistent behavior across all paths
- Fixed: Race condition when new war starts immediately after previous war ends
  - Root cause: If new war starts before old war is finalized, old war's JSON stays in temp/ and never gets processed
  - Solution: Added orphaned JSON check before main war processing - scans temp/ for old wars with different opponents
  - Logic: Any JSON file for this clan that isn't the current opponent is an old war that needs finalization
  - Result: Old wars are automatically finalized and moved to archive even when new war starts immediately
- Refactored: Simplified war finalization logic by removing war_id_changed tracking
  - Root cause: Complex tracking was needed for single-filename overwrites, but unique JSON filenames per opponent make this unnecessary
  - Changes: Removed war_id_changed, prep_transition_condition, in_war_transition_condition logic
  - New logic: finalize_needed = (state == 'war_ended' AND temp_stats AND NOT war_already_in_history)
  - Result: Much simpler, more maintainable finalization logic with same functionality
  - Benefits: Reduced complexity, fewer edge cases, clearer reasoning about when wars finalize
- Fixed: Race condition in war finalization causing "No war data found" errors
  - Root cause: save_war_object() moved JSON to archive BEFORE append_to_clan_history() read it
  - Solution: Reordered operations - file stays in temp/ until finalization completes
  - Archive move now happens in _finalize_war() AFTER history append succeeds
  - Result: Clean finalization workflow, files in correct location when needed, no race condition
- Refactored: Unified war_ended handling with _parse_war_stats_from_api() helper function
  - Eliminated 50+ lines of duplicate API parsing code (40% reduction in war_ended section)
  - Late subscription correctly triggers only when NO history file exists (checks os.path.exists)
  - Improved logging: [WAR-END] for finalization, [LATE-SUBSCRIPTION] for truly new subscriptions
- Fixed: Late attack capture and history updates for already-recorded wars
  - JSON archival now updates archived files instead of skipping (captures late attacks after war end)
  - New _update_history_with_late_attacks() function with atomic file operations (temp → backup → rename)
  - Late attacks captured in both JSON (source of truth) and history CSV (for leaderboards)
- Fixed: History file creation for late subscriptions when war already ended
  - Bot now parses war stats directly from API when war_ended and no history file exists
  - Creates history retroactively using API data, allows proper war finalization
  - Tested: Clan subscription after war end correctly creates history and finalizes war
- Modified: Log file rotation now keeps last 14 days of logs (was 4 days)
  - backupCount in TimedRotatingFileHandler increased from 3 to 13
  - Ensures two weeks of log history are retained for debugging and audits
- Added: Python script qapbot/scripts/scan_qapbot_logs.py to scan all logfiles for errors, warnings, and summarize key events. Output is compact and highlights only critical issues and event counts.
- Phase 1 implementation stable now. Continuing with phase 2. (This version commited to GitHub as stable version)

Discord-Nachricht:
📵 Benachrichtigungen für den Leaderboard-Kanal (Kurzanleitung)!

Damit euch die Push-Nachrichten des CoC-Bots nicht nerven, könnt ihr diese ganz einfach deaktivieren:

- Rechtsklick auf den Channel-Namen "leaderboard" (PC) oder lange drauf drücken beim Handy/Tablet
- „Benachrichtigungseinstellungen“ auswählen
- Wähle „Keine“

🔕 So bekommst du keine Push-Benachrichtigungen mehr aus diesem Channel.


***Here are the main available get_ methods in the coc.py library (as of 2024):***

get_clan(clan_tag)
get_player(player_tag)
get_current_war(clan_tag)
get_league_group(clan_tag)
get_war_league_season(season_id)
get_location(location_id)
get_locations()
get_clans(name=None, war_frequency=None, location_id=None, min_members=None, max_members=None, min_clan_points=None, min_clan_level=None, limit=None)
get_players(player_tags)
get_clans_by_war_league(war_league_id)
get_war_league(war_league_id)
get_war_leagues()
get_tournament(tournament_id)
get_tournaments()
get_season(season_id)
get_seasons()
get_achievement(achievement_id)
get_achievements()
get_label(label_id)
get_labels()
get_event(event_id)
get_events()
Note:
Some methods may vary depending on coc.py version and API support. The most commonly used for Clash of Clans bots are get_clan, get_player,
get_current_war, and get_league_group.


1. Error Handling & Logging
   - Use the logging module instead of print() for better control and log levels.
   - Add more granular exception handling (e.g., network errors, Discord API errors, file I/O errors).


3. Code Structure
   - Split the code into multiple modules: e.g., discord_bot.py, coc_api.py, leaderboard.py, utils.py.
   - Add docstrings and type hints for better readability and maintainability.

4. Testing
   - Add unit tests for your helper functions (e.g., file handling, leaderboard calculations).

6. Data Integrity
   - Use atomic file writes (write to a temp file, then rename) to avoid data corruption on crashes.
   - Consider using a lightweight database (like SQLite) for storing stats if the data grows.

7. Performance & Scalability
   - If you monitor many clans, consider running API calls in parallel (with care for rate limits).

8. User Feedback
   - Add more informative Discord messages (e.g., war status, time left, top attackers).
   - Optionally, DM users their personal stats.
   - DMs for war/attack reminders

9. Documentation
   - Write a README.md with setup instructions, features, and usage examples.
   - Add comments and docstrings throughout the code.

10. Deployment
    - Use a process manager (like systemd, pm2, or Docker) to keep your bot running on a server.
    - Add a requirements.txt or pyproject.toml
